Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the kinetics framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 125. The resulting operator achieves bound O(88/n^5).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the kinetics space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 125 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 88. QED.
